4b2(1): 'am ci'i rgyal po chos 'dzin dang /'khor mi 'am ci bye ba brgya stong mang po dag dang (dang)thabs   cig go/dri za'i ris kyi lha'i bu bzhi 'di lta ste/dri za yid du

4b5(1): sgra gcan dang /'khor lha ma yin bye ba brgya stong mang po dang yang thabs   cig go/nam mkha' lding gi dbang po bzhi 'di lta ste/nam mkha' lding gi
